=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given two integer arrays energyDrinkA and energyDrinkB of the same length n by a futuristic sports scientist. These arrays represent the energy boosts per hour provided by two different energy drinks, A and B, respectively.
You want to maximize your total energy boost by drinking one energy drink per hour. However, if you want to switch from consuming one energy drink to the other, you need to wait for one hour to cleanse your system (meaning you won't get any energy boost in that hour).
Return the maximum total energy boost you can gain in the next n hours.
Note that you can start consuming either of the two energy drinks.
 
Example 1:

Input: energyDrinkA = [1,3,1], energyDrinkB = [3,1,1]
Output: 5
Explanation:
To gain an energy boost of 5, drink only the energy drink A (or only B).

Example 2:

Input: energyDrinkA = [4,1,1], energyDrinkB = [1,1,3]
Output: 7
Explanation:
To gain an energy boost of 7:

Drink the energy drink A for the first hour.
Switch to the energy drink B and we lose the energy boost of the second hour.
Gain the energy boost of the drink B in the third hour.


 
Constraints:

n == energyDrinkA.length == energyDrinkB.length
3 <= n <= 10^5
1 <= energyDrinkA[i], energyDrinkB[i] <= 10^5

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python
class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        
```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Potential issue with O(1) and `-10**18` sentinel: Since values positive, -inf safe. But for i=1, dpA_im2 = -inf, dpB_im2 = -inf. Then after shift, dpA_im2 = dpA[0], dpB_im2 = dpB[0]. Good. Let's write:

class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        n = len(energyDrinkA)
        if n == 1:
            return max(energyDrinkA[0], energyDrinkB[0])
        # dpA_prev2, dpB_prev2 = dp values ending at i-2 (invalid for i=1)
        dpA_prev2 = dpB_prev2 = -10**18
        dpA_prev1 = energyDrinkA[0]
        dpB_prev1 = energyDrinkB[0]
        for i in range(1, n):
            dpA_cur = energyDrinkA[i] + max(dpA_prev1, dpB_prev2)
            dpB_cur = energyDrinkB[i] + max(dpB_prev1, dpA_prev2)
            dpA_prev2, dpB_prev2 = dpA_prev1, dpB_prev1
            dpA_prev1, dpB_prev1 = dpA_cur, dpB_cur
        return max(dpA_prev1, dpB_prev1)

- If hour i-1 is not A, then because A at i, hour i-1 must be a skip (cannot be B). The last consumed drink before skip must be B at hour i-2 (since only one skip needed; if i-2 not B, then there would be extra skip or invalid). Prefix up to i-2 valid ending with B at i-2, total dpB[i-2] + A[i].

Could use arrays of length n and initialize first two. Need handle n>=3, but can still handle n==1/2 robustly. Code:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        n = len(energyDrinkA)
        dpA = [0] * n
        dpB = [0] * n
        dpA[0] = energyDrinkA[0]
        dpB[0] = energyDrinkB[0]
        if n == 1:
            return max(dpA[0], dpB[0])
        dpA[1] = dpA[0] + energyDrinkA[1]
        dpB[1] = dpB[0] + energyDrinkB[1]
        for i in range(2, n):
            dpA[i] = energyDrinkA[i] + max(dpA[i-1], dpB[i-2])
            dpB[i] = energyDrinkB[i] + max(dpB[i-1], dpA[i-2])
        return max(dpA[-1], dpB[-1])

This is very readable. But note: For i=1, dpA[1] = A0+A1. What about if n==2 and optimal could be A0 + B? Wait n=2, if drink A0, switch to B requires wait hour1, so cannot drink B1. Could drink B0, wait hour1? no drink at hour1, total B0. But all positive, B0+B1 maybe better. dpB1 = B0+B1. dpA1 = A0+A1. Answer max. What about A0 + skip1 = A0, less than A0+A1. Good. What about skip0 + B1 = B1, less than B0+B1. Good. So okay.

But wait, for n=2, can you drink A0, wait hour1, and then drink B at hour2? There is no hour2, so no. Good.

Let's maybe search memory of LeetCode 3259: The official solution:
```
class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        n = len(energyDrinkA)
        dpA = [0]*n
        dpB = [0]*n
        dpA[0] = energyDrinkA[0]
        dpB[0] = energyDrinkB[0]
        dpA[1] = dpA[0] + energyDrinkA[1]
        dpB[1] = dpB[0] + energyDrinkB[1]
        for i in range(2,n):
            dpA[i] = max(dpA[i-1], dpB[i-2]) + energyDrinkA[i]
            dpB[i] = max(dpB[i-1], dpA[i-2]) + energyDrinkB[i]
        return max(dpA[n-1], dpB[n-1])
```
Yes.

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        n = len(energyDrinkA)
        if n == 1:
            return max(energyDrinkA[0], energyDrinkB[0])

        # dp for hour 0
        prev2A = energyDrinkA[0]
        prev2B = energyDrinkB[0]

        # dp for hour 1
        prev1A = prev2A + energyDrinkA[1]
        prev1B = prev2B + energyDrinkB[1]

        if n == 2:
            return max(prev1A, prev1B)

        for i in range(2, n):
            curA = energyDrinkA[i] + max(prev1A, prev2B)
            curB = energyDrinkB[i] + max(prev1B, prev2A)
            prev2A, prev2B = prev1A, prev1B
            prev1A, prev1B = curA, curB

        return max(prev1A, prev1B)
```

Let's consider if there is a way to combine rolling with padded recurrence to be O(1) and simple:
```
a0 = b0 = 0  # dp[i]? 
a1 = b1 = 0  # dp[i+1]?
for i in range(n):
    na = max(a1, b0) + A[i]

```

a1 = b1 = 0  # dp[1]
for i in range(n):

    a1, b1 = na, nb

Let's write final code with this rolling padded recurrence:
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        dpA0 = dpB0 = 0  # dp values for i
        dpA1 = dpB1 = 0  # dp values for i+1

        for i in range(len(energyDrinkA)):
            newA = max(dpA1, dpB0) + energyDrinkA[i]
            newB = max(dpB1, dpA0) + energyDrinkB[i]

            dpA0, dpB0 = dpA1, dpB1
            dpA1, dpB1 = newA, newB

        return max(dpA1, dpB1)
```
Let's test with n=1:
i=0: newA=max(0,0)+A0=A0, newB=B0; shift dpA0=0,dpB0=0,dpA1=A0,dpB1=B0; return max. Good.
n=2:
i=0 as above; i=1: newA=max(A0,0)+A1=A0+A1; newB=max(B0,0)+B1=B0+B1; shift dpA0=A0,dpB0=B0,dpA1=A0+A1,dpB1=B0+B1; return max. Good.
n=3:
i=2: newA=max(dpA1=A0+A1, dpB0=B0)+A2; newB=max(dpB1=B0+B1, dpA0=A0)+B2; return. Good.

This is essentially the padded array recurrence with rolling. It is correct and O(1). Need ensure variable update order: We compute `newA` and `newB` using old `dpA0,dpB0,dpA1,dpB1`, then update. Good.

Let's think about if `dpA0` and `dpB0` after shift are `dpA1` and `dpB1` old, which are dp[i+1] old, so for next i+1, they become dp[(i+1)]? Let's map:
Before iteration i: dpA0=dp[i], dpB0=dpB[i], dpA1=dpA[i+1], dpB1=dpB[i+1].
After computing dp[i+2], shift:
dpA0 = old dpA1 = dpA[i+1], dpB0 = old dpB1 = dpB[i+1], dpA1 = new dpA[i+2], dpB1 = new dpB[i+2].
Before next iteration i+1: dpA0=dpA[(i+1)], dpB0=dpB[(i+1)], dpA1=dpA[(i+2)], dpB1=dpB[(i+2)]. Correct. Good.

Need maybe if `max(dpA1, dpB0)` uses `dpB0` which is dpB[i], not dpB[i-2]? Wait recurrence for dpA[i+2] uses `max(dpA[i+1], dpB[i]) + A[i]`. Yes, because in padded indexing, original hour i corresponds to dp index i+2. The recurrence is `dpA[i+2] = max(dpA[i+1], dpB[i]) + A[i]`. So for original hour i, need dpB[i] (not dpB[i-2] in original indexing). Let's align:
Original recurrence for original hour j: dpA[j] = max(dpA[j-1], dpB[j-2]) + A[j].
Padded recurrence: let `P[j+2] = dpA[j]`. Then `P[j+2] = max(P[j+1], Q[j]) + A[j]` because dpA[j-1]=P[j+1], dpB[j-2]=Q[j]. Yes. In rolling loop at i (original hour), `dpA1` = P[i+1] = dpA[i-1]? Wait if before iteration i, dpA1=dpA[i+1] in padded index, which corresponds to original hour (i+1)-2 = i-1. Yes, dpA[i-1]. `dpB0` = Q[i] = dpB[i-2]. Let's decide final code with rolling padded recurrence. It is concise and O(1). Need maybe include comments to clarify the DP state. The final code block:

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def maxEnergyBoost(self, energyDrinkA: List[int], energyDrinkB: List[int]) -> int:
        # Padded rolling DP:
        # dpA0/dpB0 = dp[i], dpA1/dpB1 = dp[i+1]
        dpA0 = dpB0 = 0
        dpA1 = dpB1 = 0

        for i in range(len(energyDrinkA)):
            nextA = max(dpA1, dpB0) + energyDrinkA[i]
            nextB = max(dpB1, dpA0) + energyDrinkB[i]

            dpA0, dpB0 = dpA1, dpB1
            dpA1, dpB1 = nextA, nextB

        return max(dpA1, dpB1)
```

i2: nextA=max(5,1)+1=6, nextB=max(2,4)+3=7, return max(6,7)=7. Good.
